# Seamless co-op parity: Elden Ring Seamless Co-op (ERSC) vs. bbport party co-op

Master tracking table. The model is Elden Ring Seamless Co-op (LukeYui): friends-only password sessions,
no tether, the session survives deaths and bosses, and everyone in the party progresses as if
playing solo. Update a row whenever its status changes (date + evidence).

**Two numbers per row (since 2026-10-15, confid, after the independent score audit
`agents/scoreaudit_report.md`):**

* **Plan done** (the `%` column of the tables below): how much of the row's plan is built and seen
  working. 100 = the plan's target (ERSC's behaviour, a sane BB equivalent, or a recorded user
  decision) is implemented and verified live at least once; code written but not verified live counts
  at most 60. Judgement calls by the agent that did the work.
* **ERSC parity (evidence-weighted)** (the table "Evidence and ERSC parity" after the Summary): the
  plan score capped by what the evidence can carry and by what ERSC actually does:
  * a row whose evidence is only single-PC loopback (127.0.0.1, with or without test aids) is capped at
    **92** (95 until 2026-10-21; lowered after playtest 2, where about a third of the rows the real
    session exercised failed although the table had them at 95); `real-network-1PC` evidence (one PC
    over the real Internet, a real router, the guest through a VPN exit) caps it at **94**; only a real
    two-PC session over the Internet on the current build can take it above 94;
  * a row that **failed in a real session** and is fixed on one PC only is scored well below the cap
    (84-88) until a real session confirms the fix;
  * a row that is 100 **by decision** (a user decision or a redefinition that differs from ERSC: the
    party respawning with the host, extra loads, max 4 players, one shared drop roll) is capped at **95**
    (and by its evidence cap) with the difference written next to it;
  * unit-only evidence keeps the 60 cap.
  The area and overall parity numbers are unweighted means of these capped row scores.

Every row also carries an **evidence tag**: `loopback-live` (live runs on one PC driven by real game
flows / pad input, no aids that force the outcome), `loopback-scripted+aids` (live on one PC, driven by
test aids: director warps, ForceDead, forced flags, invincibility, process kills), `unit` (tests only),
`two-PC-real` (a real session on two PCs over the Internet; none on the current build; `two-PC-real FAILED`
when the row failed there), `real-network-1PC` (one PC over the real Internet: a real router and UPnP, the
guest's exe split-tunnelled through a VPN exit with another public IP; not a second household), `by-decision`
(see above); and the **ERSC claim's source**: `FAQ-confirmed` (ersc-docs.github.io FAQ / settings
page), `secondary` (the Nexus description as pasted, guides, videos; not re-checked), `assumed`
(marked *(verify)*, inferred, or a self-defined target where ERSC has no equivalent).

The player-facing summary is [docs/COOP.md](../COOP.md) (How it plays /
Deaths / Coming next). Whenever a row changes what a player experiences, update that section in the
same commit: move items from "Coming next" to "How it plays" once verified live.

ERSC sources: the Nexus description (v2.0.0, pasted by the user 2026-10-11), the Nightreign mod's
description (scaling semantics), an ExitLag guide (secondary; it says 4 players where Nexus says 6, Nexus wins), the mod's docs site (https://ersc-docs.github.io/: install/settings page and FAQ, read
2026-10-11). The Nexus page (nexusmods.com/eldenring/mods/510) blocks automated fetches. ERSC
behaviour marked *(verify)* is from memory or weak sources and is not confirmed by the docs.

## Bloodborne rule (user, 2026-10-11) - overrides ERSC where they differ

Seamless co-op must feel like a party making progress the way single-player does: no unnecessary
loading screens, summons, lost progress or respawns, and every player is free to build their
character exactly as in single-player play. Every row below is judged against this rule too: a row is
only 100% when it meets it.

## Design decisions (user, 2026-10-11) - binding

| Topic | Decision |
|---|---|
| Travel by lamp / headstone / Hunter's Mark | Ask at the lamp each time: "Travel alone" or "Travel with party" |
| Death outside a boss fight | Every player, host included, respawns at their OWN last lamp (even in another map); the others play on |
| Death during a boss fight | Limbo: spectate until the boss dies, the party wipes, or someone rests. Boss fights use a SHARED lamp: after a boss-fight death or wipe everyone respawns at the same lamp (changed 2026-10-11) |
| Echoes on death | Like single-player for everyone: echoes drop into a recoverable bloodstain |
| Item pickups and enemy drops | Instanced per player: each player can take each world item once into their own save, and every enemy drop is per player too (changed 2026-10-11), from ONE shared roll: the same items for everyone from a given kill, each player its own copy (user, 2026-10-14: "roll should be the same for all always") |
| NPC quest choices | Shared: the first choice made by anyone applies to the host's world and every player's save |
| Guest behind the host in story | Guest's own save catches up fully (bosses, Blood Moon, lamps, shortcuts) |
| Saves | Everyone uses a separate party save; single-player saves untouched |
| Rest at a lamp | Resets enemies for everyone (ERSC); players in boss rooms are pulled out |
| Enemy scaling default | Harder than vanilla (ERSC-like boss boosts), adjustable in Party settings |
| Death debuff ("rot") | Implemented, OFF by default since 0.7.2 (user, 2026-10-22; was ON), the host turns it on in System > Party rules: Rot, with cleanse rules (boss kill, the killer's death, the echoes retrieved) |
| Chalice dungeons | Any player can perform a ritual; the party enters that dungeon together |
| Host offline | Host needed; the shared world exists only while the host plays |
| Hunter's Dream | Always shared: everyone in the Dream is in the host's Dream together |
| Cutscenes | Play for everyone in the party |
| Joining during a boss fight / cutscene | Joiner waits outside (host's last lamp / fog) until it ends |
| Party size | Max 4 players (host + 3) supported; 5-player test runs allowed (2026-10-12) to try host + 4 |
| Friendly fire / PvP, invasions | Not part of 100%. PvP / friendly fire the host can switch on and off is the "100%+" row I1 (built in 0.6.2, off by default); invasions stay out |

## Summary

| Area | Plan done | ERSC parity (evidence-weighted) |
|---|---|---|
| A. Session & joining | 99% | 94% |
| B. World & travel | 100% | 92% |
| C. Death & respawn | 100% | 90% |
| D. Progress (anyone can progress) | 95% | 86% |
| E. Services & UI | 100% | 90% |
| F. Network & robustness | 95% | 88% |
| G. BB-specific (chalice, insight, bath) | 100% | 90% |
| H. Bloodborne rule (feels like SP in a party) | 100% | 90% |
| **Overall (unweighted mean of the 49 scored rows; "not planned" rows and section I excluded)** | **~98%** | **~90%** |

## Evidence and ERSC parity (evidence-weighted)

Per row: the plan score (the `%` column below), the evidence behind it, where the ERSC behaviour it is
compared with comes from, and the ERSC parity score (capped as described at the top of this file).
Since 2026-11-01 (parity4p078) the strongest evidence is the 4-PC playtest of that day: host plus three guests on four separate PCs over the real Internet through the Nightwatch VPN, our 0.7.7 party code inside the Nightwatch build, about 5 hours, one host game crash (exit 139) with a restart. The user counts it as `two-PC-real` for passes as well as failures, although the build is a derivative one (the 0.7.2 rule said never a pass); to stay honest the rows it passed score at most 96 (one session), the by-decision rows 95, and every row it failed stays tagged `two-PC-real FAILED` and scored 80-88 with the 0.7.8 fix noted as not re-tested by players. Rows the session did not exercise keep their score and got no entry (A6, A8, C4, C7, G1). Before it: no row had `two-PC-real` evidence on the current build. Since 2026-10-22 (parity072), player reports from two-PC sessions on a derivative build that carries our ~0.7.1 party code count as `two-PC-real FAILED` for the rows they broke (never as a pass: the build is not ours). The real Internet sessions so far: playtest 1
(0.6.0, 2026-10-13, 22 bugs) and playtest 2 (0.6.9, 2026-10-20, two PCs, 2 h; the rows it failed are
tagged `two-PC-real FAILED`). The real-network VPN test of 2026-10-21 (`agents/vpntest/realnet_runs/20261021_1929`,
direct phase and a first relay run, 37 of 39 checks; `20261021_2117`, relay phase 15/15) ran the current build
over the real Internet on one PC: a real router with UPnP, the guest's exe split-tunnelled through a VPN exit
with another public IP; it is `real-network-1PC` evidence, not a second household. A third group's logs were
on a derivative build whose joins a version mismatch refused: no co-op evidence, not counted. An independent
audit of these scores (agents/scoreaudit_report.md, 2026-10-15) put the parity a real two-PC session would
show at ~85% (78-90%); the caps here are the rule, that estimate is the bet.

## A. Session & joining

| # | Feature | ERSC | bbport now | % | Evidence / next |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A1 | Friends-only, password, no randoms | `cooppassword` in ersc_settings.ini (must match for all); vanilla matchmaking off | Serverless P2P with a password (stored DPAPI-protected), host address + port | 100 | party_flow; internet (NAT) untested -> F2 2026-10-15 (parab a1a, live, one session): host with a password, guest with a wrong one: refused at its title with the game-style notice "Party: Wrong party password (or an old party code ...)" and what to do (screenshot), it plays online alone in its own world; with the right password set (test aid `BB_PARTY_TEST_REJOIN_PASSWORD`, as typed in System > Party) and Rejoin the host it title-joined the host's world 8.2 s later (host `cooperators 0 -> 1`). 100 = matches ERSC: friends-only (no matchmaking: the only peers are the party link's), the password checked at the link (Argon2 key, lockout F3), a clear refusal with the way out. Internet NAT reachability is F2's row. 2026-10-21 (parity071): real-network VPN test: title join with the party code and password over the Internet (one PC, a VPN exit). Parity 95 -> 94 (the new caps: loopback 92, one-PC real network 94). |
| A2 | Separate co-op save | Own save file (`save_file_extension`, e.g. .co2 vs vanilla .sl2) | Separate party save, single-player save untouched | 100 | party_flow, ingame_flow.md 2026-10-15 (parab3 a4t1, live, one session): 8 party joins / leaves with saves on both games; afterwards every file of both instances' single-player `user\savedata` was byte-identical to the test save it was copied from (md5 of all 14 files), while `user\savedata_party` was written during the session and backed up at the party start (`user\save_backups\savedata_party`). 100 = ERSC's separate co-op save: the party process picks the party save itself (no setting to forget) and never opens the single-player saves for writing. |
| A3 | Start / join in game | Tiny Great Pot creates the session; Effigy of Meleina joins from anywhere in the world, spawning near the host; mod items are granted on a grace rest | Title: New / Join / Continue party game (a quick restart into the party game: patches, imports and the save folder are chosen at boot); in-game System > Party: Join / Leave / Rejoin; Party page values update live | 100 | 2026-10-11 flowlive: live runs fresh / continue / Leave + Join party through the real menus (ingame_flow.md 7), live value columns. Open: the restart (exit 75) stays by design (ingame_flow.md 2) 2026-10-15 (parab3 a7t1, live, current defaults: title join + direct summon): in the world a guest's *Leave the party* (the action of System > Party > Leave, pressed by `BB_PARTY_STATUS_TEST`) took it out in 0.4 s (`client -> leaving -> idle`) and home with one load; *Rejoin* 15 s later started a fresh link and the direct summon put it back in the host's world 1.5 s after its request, with 3 other players there (host `cooperators 2 -> 3`). 100: ERSC starts co-op through its own launcher (a separate co-op process and save) and joins in the world with an item; here the title's Party page starts the party process (the restart is ERSC's launcher step, by design) and joining / leaving / rejoining in the world are menu rows, verified live. 2026-10-15 (parx2, post-merge smoke, live): a guest's Leave + Rejoin right after a boss kill (no load between) was KICKED by the host's game 0.9 s after each join (smoke3: twice, `KickoutRoomMember` reason 0xFF000019): BlockClear2 locks the session (SprjSessionManager +0x118 via LockSession 0x178D7B0) and, with K10 keeping the session, the host's session update 0x1ED0B20 kicked every later joiner (the guest's OnJoinClearedRoom). Fixed (K10b: that call 0x1ED14FB is skipped while the party is on); live (fin): Leave 15 s after Gascoigne's kill, Rejoin, `K10b ... skipped`, joined 1.9 s after the request, host `cooperators 1 -> 2`. 100 kept. 2026-10-21 (parity071): real-network VPN test: title join, Party > Leave and Rejoin over the Internet. Parity 95 -> 94 (the new caps: loopback 92, one-PC real network 94). 2026-11-05 (parity079, 0.7.9): title sign-in hold for a slow ss.info (smoke `cas`, loopback). |
| A4 | Join speed | Seconds after both are in the world *(verify)* | Guest world steady -> first bell 1.2 s (was 6.9 s: pre-ring spacing 0.8 s per side), world up -> summoned 10.5 s, loading gone -> summoned 6 s; Continue: host hosts 5 s after its restart | 100 | 2026-10-11 flowlive (timestamped runs, join_timing.md). Later 2026-10-11: JoinRoom -> client 0.9 s (first RTO 300 ms), client -> loading 0.8 s (prologue 0.1 s). Left: bell -> sign 2 s (0.5 s broke joins, race not found yet), the host-world load, Continue still loads the own world first; one slow sign was a waiting guest's needless load (fixed: waiting guests no longer follow same-map host travels or the host's death); the full-screen item cards seen on guests are the game's loading-screen tips; Continue -> host world in one load: on by default since 2026-10-12 (title join; 4 live 2-client runs: one load, host and guest see each other, Leave goes home; join_timing.md); 2026-10-12: first 2-PC LAN session took 63 s because two summons failed (host never sent the summon data) and each retry waited for the guest's 30 s sign resend: a failed summon now re-offers the sign at once and the summon window is 8 s (was 15 s) 2026-10-15 (parab a5b / a5c, live, direct summon default on): a guest in another map: world steady -> bell 0.3 s -> the host's room 0.25 s -> client 3.0-3.1 s -> ONE loading screen (6.1-6.2 s) -> beside the host (0.5 m): 12.3 / 12.8 s in all; title join (a1a, b1a): in the host's world 5.6-8.2 s after the title join started. What is left is the real load and the transport's first connect. 95 not 100: the bell sign path's 2 s timer (the 0.5 s race of 2026-10-11) is still there; it is only the fallback when a direct summon is refused. 2026-10-15 (parab2 a4ds / a4sp, join_timing.md): the 2 s timer also paces the direct summon (the game takes the room only into its bell's own summon entry, which comes up when the timer fires): with `BB_PARTY_SIGN_DELAY=0.5` (new test switch) request -> client fell from 3.0 to 1.5 s in 4 of 5 rejoins in one session, but the 5th stalled after client (6) (no host-world load for 6 s), and a second session with 12 s between joins stalled the same way on its 2nd join (a4ds2), the 2026-10-11 symptom without the host's sign polling in the loop; the sign path with the 0.5 s timer and the new board hold (a sign is shown 2 s after the ring) joined 7 of 7. So the race is on the guest's side (a join that reaches it well before ~2 s after its ring), not in the sign path; the default stays 2.0 s. 95 not 100: the guest's flow-state-1 stall is not reverse-engineered yet. 2026-10-15 (parab3 a4t1-a4t3, join_timing.md): the guest's stall after client (6) is the game's Lua `SummonReloadStart` (0x1389440): it loads only with presentation count >= 2, otherwise it clears the summon type (GSM +0x16F0) and the join sits in flow 2 with no load. A flow-2 watchdog now runs the game's join start again (live with `BB_PARTY_TEST_JOIN_STALL`: flow 2, type 0, then `A4: ... the game's join start again`, the load, in the world 3.5 s after the client role). The bell timer is 0.5 s by default: 23 of 24 direct-summon joins in three sessions took 1.5-1.6 s from the request to the client role (was 3.0 s), the host-world load ended 3.7-5.6 s later (ring -> beside the host ~5.3 s in the same map); the one miss was the host's game closing its empty room 27 ms after serving it (`Room not found`): a served room now gets 10 s more. No natural stall in 24 joins. 100: seconds from the ring to the host's world on every join path (title join, direct summon, bell sign fallback). 2026-10-15 (parab4 a8a, live, one session): the served-room extension seen: the guest left and pressed Rejoin 1 s later; its first request came while the host's game was still ending the old session (refused, `a fresh room follows`), the next 1.5 s later found the host's empty room with 1.4 s left (`this host's empty room had 1.4 s left before the game closes it: 10 s for the joiner`), the guest got the room and reported joined 1.0 s later; no `Room not found`, no bell fallback. 2026-10-15 (parx2, live a6s): a title join that asked 0.16 s after the host's own lamp warp got a room carrying the map the host was leaving (the Dream) and stood there apart for 225 s; the host now serves a title join only once no stage request is armed (its load first). the rendered guest left and rejoined by direct summon (in again 3.8 s after Rejoin, `cooperators 3 -> 4` on every guest), then **both headless guests crashed 2 and 7 s later** in the game's chr threads (CSChrThread1 / 2) reading a peer's type-6 packet queue (0x1A6D390 -> 0x1789200 -> 0x17910F0: a null memcpy source, and "heap not found" on a free: the queue was freed or changed under them); their restarts' title joins then failed (no snapshot in 20 s) and the host's game kicked the rejoined guest. 90 stays: four players by title join are live, a 4th player's rejoin crashed the other two (headless only so far; four_players.md 9). 2026-10-15 (parab4, fixed, four_players.md 9.1): the crash was the **bystander**: a guest that sees another guest leave ended its task but kept its PlayerIns in the ChrSet (cooperators stayed 2); at the rejoin that old chr matched the member again next to the new one, and two chr threads popped one peer's type-6 queue at once (the game's queues 0x1789200 / 0x17910F0 have no lock). Found live with new diagnostics in a 3-player run (a7b: `two chrs read peer entry`, `cooperators 2 -> 3` with 3 players, then a crash in a task step), so 3 players and rendered bystanders had it too. Fix: a guest removes a departed member's leftover chr 2 s after the leave (the host's way: enterleave leave, presentation slot release, WorldChrMan remove); safety nets: one lock on every peer-queue push / pop, and the newer of two chrs reading one peer keeps the updates. Live after the fix: a7e (3 players, host + rendered + headless bystander, three leave / rejoin cycles in one session): the bystander logs the removal 2.5 s after each leave, `cooperators 2 -> 1 -> 2` every cycle, no duplicate reader, no crash; a7f (**4 players**, host + rendered + 2 headless, the rendered guest left / rejoined twice and a headless guest once): host `cooperators 3 -> 2 -> 3` three times, both bystanders remove the leaver each time, never more than 3 cooperators anywhere, no crash, screenshots show all four with three party rows on every game after each rejoin. 100: four players live, joins, leaves and rejoins of any member while the others stay. **2026-10-25 (0.7.6): two-PC-real FAILED (player reports from real sessions on Nightwatch Co-op 0.2.3 (our 0.7.4 party code), week of 2026-10-19/25): a 4-player lobby, 2 of the 3 guests stuck loading (their UDP was not heard by the host); netfix de450888 (VPN path kept, own-network peers kept, clearer text) is unit tests + a join smoke only** |

The game's 25 s transport idle check is not in the transport's config block; ~15 s needs more RE. 2026-10-14 (pt1join): a restarted member's sign hold ends once the host's game has cleared its old peer (playtest 1: "a crashed member's connection is still held" refused the new run); live crash rejoin under dsl: direct summon 2.9 s after the request, cooperators 0 -> 1 ~20 s after the crash 2026-10-14 (pt1follow): no premature "no arrival within 180 s" after a guest restart (the travel mark is dropped once B2's re-join kept the room); live restart: rejoined by direct summon, no such line 2026-10-14 (par_c6, live, one session + one verify session, loopback, `--crash-plan`): **host crash** while idle in the world: the guest goes home at once (`host lost ... home now`), the restarted host Continues (crash restart), the guest is `back in the party after 9.9 s (the host restarted: a new session)` and rejoins by direct summon 7 s after the host's world is up: cooperators 0 -> 1 **33.5 s after the crash**, placed beside the host (screenshots). **Host crash during its lamp travel**: the guest followed the crashed host's travel alone, then gathered back to the restarted host's Dream lamp while the host moved on, and waited out the gather's 90 s (rejoined 133 s after the crash); fixed after the run, not re-run: a B1 fallback with the host's link down does not follow the travel, and once at the last gather's map a host that moved on is gathered to after 10 s. **Guest crash** (twice, once beside the host, once 25 s after a rejoin): title join straight beside the host (0.5 m), cooperators 0 -> 1 35 s after the crash (~15 s boot + Continue). Title-join race fixed live: a host lamp travel 4 s after sending its room to a title joiner left the guest in a B1 fallback (re-summoned 50 s later); the travel now waits for the joiner (`host lamp warp ... held: a member is joining from its title`, released 1.9 s later, one join). Next: a crash during a boss fight; the host's restart time (~15 s boot) 2026-10-14 (verify_c6, live, one session with 3 ordered crashes, screenshots): **host crash during its lamp travel** (Cleric Beast lamp -> Dream, killed 1.5 s into the loading screen): guest `host lost ... home now`, `FALLBACK to B1 ... the host's link is down: its travel is not followed`, home load to its own world, then `gather: arrived at the last gather's map ..., the host has moved on to m21_00_00_00: gathering again` as soon as the restarted host was in the world (no 90 s wait), cooperators 0 -> 1 **31.5 s after the crash** (was 133 s), beside the host. **Guest crash during a boss fight** (Cleric Beast bar up on both): the restarted guest's title join was refused (`the host is in a boss fight (it waits in its own world ...)`, A11), it Continued at home; the host fought alone and died, `A11: ... ringing again`, guest summoned 0.5 m from the host 51 s after the crash. Guest crash right after a give while summoned: title join beside the host 20 s later. Seen: the host's session went leaving -> idle 12 s after the guest's crash in the fight (BeginLeaveSession from +0x1789f99) and was hosted again after its death; no effect on the rejoin 2026-10-15 (robust1, live, 2 runs, screenshots): **host crash: the guest waits for the host** - home at once (one load), "Waiting for the host to come back (N s)..." in the corner and on the Party page, the restarted host's link back 6.0 s after the crash, its world up 18.0 s, the guest in its room 18.6 s after the crash (was 33.5 s in par_c6), beside it (2.3 m), 2 loads in all; a 3-minute cap (`BB_PARTY_HOST_WAIT_S`) ends the wait with a notice and leaves the party (unit only). network_robustness.md section 8. Not possible: no home load (the crashed room is gone; the game cannot move a phantom between rooms) 2026-10-15 (parfin3 s3b, live, one session, `--crash-plan`, screenshots every 3 s): **guest crash rejoin 16.6 s / 16.9 s** from the kill to the host's `cooperators 0 -> 1` (was 35 s in par_c6), the guest in the world beside the host (0.7-0.8 m, screenshot) 26.6 s / 27.0 s after the kill: restart 5.5 s (run.bat's wait 3 s -> 1 s), the host's room at once, the snapshot 1.0-1.1 s after the join, then the FROM sign-in hold (~5 s) and the game's own load (~12 s). **Found and fixed**: the F5 outage replay is keyed by the peer's address and port, which a restarted game keeps, so the host replayed the dead session's reliable records into the new game right after its join (4-player runs a7b / a7t1: `outage resume ... heard again after 7.6 s: 14 reliable record(s) sent again`, then `title join: FAILED (no snapshot within 20 s)`, own world, a re-summon 35-50 s later); the C6 release now forgets that source's replay state (`C6: its outage-replay state forgotten (5 entries): no replay into its restarted game`, both crashes: no replay, snapshot in 1 s). A failed title join now gives up after 12 s instead of 20 s. **Host-wait cap live**: host crash without a restart, guest `BB_PARTY_HOST_WAIT_S=30`: "Waiting for the host to come back (N s)..." counting in the corner (screenshots 7 s, 28 s), then `host wait: the host did not come back within 30 s: leaving the party` 32 s after the crash, the guest on in its own world. Open: the FROM sign-in hold before Continue (~5 s; what starts the game's ss.info fetch needs RE). 2026-10-15 (parlast, live, 3 sessions, screenshots; network_robustness.md 8b): **the FROM sign-in reverse-engineered**: every HTTP answer takes 0-1 ms and NP auth answers at once (our side; a 25 ms cap on the game's `sceHttpWaitRequest` was tried and changed nothing), the FROM client's update 0x1E7DF20 runs every frame (dt 0.033 s, traced), and the pace is the title's own menu-step chain (0x1C37B30: stages 0x1C48F80 NP auth, 0x1C492C0 ss.info, 0x1C48AA0 login, 0x1C43EE0 / 0x1C485D0 chara id / notice; each starts when the menu framework resumes the previous step's future, ~1 s apart: ss.info -> login 1.03 s, login -> notice 0.90 s). Live (g3b, `BB_PARTY_TRACE_FROM_UPDATE`): kill -> boot 2.4 s, link 4.6 s, title takes a press 7.0 s, ss.info stage 10.1 s, sign-in over 11.9 s (1.8 s), Continue's load 14.1 s, host `cooperators 0 -> 1` ~15 s; combo: two guest crashes rejoined 14 s / 15 s after the kill, beside the host (0.5 m). Not shortened, by decision: Continue during the sign-in leaves the game offline (the hold ends the frame the last answer is in), and the remaining seconds are FROM's own boot, title and load, which every rejoin after a crash pays in ERSC too (there the player relaunches and re-summons by hand); patching the shared menu-step scheduler for ~3 s is not worth the risk. 100: the crashed guest is back in the room in ~15 s with no player action, the host restarts and is waited for, both live. 2026-10-16 (confid, post-merge smoke s2, live, 3 players: host + guest 1 rendered, guest 2 headless-lite, twice on two builds): **a guest crash right after a boss kill (Cleric Beast) does not rejoin cleanly**: the restarted guest's title join joined the room but got no world data (`title join: FAILED (no snapshot within 12 s; role 4, flow 0)`), Continued at home, and ~38 s after the crash the host's game kicked the OTHER guest (`KickoutRoomMember ... reason 0xff000020`, its stale-member timeout at 0xC99FA2); both were back by direct summon only 74-86 s after the crash. Lifting the boss-clear session lock (K10d, 0x1384B89 skipped while the party is on) did not change it; open, being narrowed down by `repeat_party.py c6 --players 3` (with / without a boss kill). 100 -> 90 until fixed. 2026-10-16 (smokefix, live smoke s2, 3 players): **cause found**: the host released its game's connection to the crashed game (NrDropSilentPeers), but the OTHER guest's game still held one, and that guest's F5 outage replay sent the dead session's reliable records into the restarted game (guest 2: `outage resume: 127.0.0.1:...: heard again after 6.4 s: 12 reliable record(s) sent again` the moment the restarted game joined); the restarted game's join then never reached the host's member task (stuck at StartMultiPlayNotifyWait, step 1), `title join: FAILED`, and ~44 s later the host's stale-member timeout kicked a member (0xFF000020; with that task's slot unset it could be the other guest). Not the boss kill. **Fix** (confid's unmerged 43078a41, taken over, plus smokefix): every guest releases its game's connection to a member whose link was lost and who is back at its title (a restart); a plain Leave + Rejoin no longer triggers it (the first version also released a live game on a Leave + Rejoin: the other guest was kicked 15 s later, smoke run1). Also a bystander guest removes the crashed member's old chr (frozen copies piled up per crash). Smoke run1 (agents/smokefix/smoke/run1): guest crash right after the Cleric Beast kill back in the room **30.0 s** after the kill (was 80-91 s), no failed title join, nobody kicked. 90 -> 100. 2026-10-21 (parity071): the % cell said 90 although smokefix had moved it to 100 (2026-10-16, change log); corrected to 100. |

| D3 | Guest actions change the host's world | Yes *(verify)* | Guest levers / gates / doors: the host's game gets the guest's ObjAct kick natively (logged) and the object animates in the host's world; the guest's flag changes in its copy of the host's world go to the host as EVENT `world_action` (whitelisted shortcut / world-state / key / NPC flags, host re-validates against its map, rate limit, fuzzed) and into the guest's own save; kick replay when the host's game missed it; a guest's finished lift ride goes up as `lift_set` / `lift_clr` and the host writes it when the lift's map block is not loaded in its game (lifts071) | 98 | 2026-10-21 lifts071 (live, `agents/lifts071/runs`, l1-l10 + smoke `lf`; every game logs the lift's flags each tick (`log_flag=`) and the car's animation state (`log_objanim=`), EMEVD trace of the lift events): **lifts**. How they sync: every game runs its own copy of a lift's events; a step into the car or a call lever on one game starts the copy of every game that has the lift's block loaded within ~0.05 s (the game's own sync), and a copy whose car is far away (object not active) ends its ride at once with the same flags. Live, Central Yharnam lift 2411320, 3 players (l5): the guest rode down alone (-44.3 -> -58.1 in 5 s, the host 131 m away: its copy ended at once; 12410350 = 0 on all three games), the host called the car up with the top lever while the guest watched from the bottom (anims 6 / 3 / 7 on both, 10 s, both screens), the host rode down with the guest watching (screenshots: the host in the car on the guest's screen, the guest outside the open car on the host's), the guest pressed the call lever while the host stood in the arrived car: "Device is not currently operable." on the guest (the down event waits until everyone left the car, as in single player), the lift unchanged on both; all games' flags equal after every ride. The guest falling down the shaft in l1-l4 (and dlast's car going back up) was the test's warp straight into the car from 131 m (the car's object not active yet, its copy ended at once while the guest stood on nothing); walking up first rides normally. **The invisible-door / blocked-lift bug (found, fixed)**: a game with the lift's block streamed out never runs its copy, and lift flags were never world actions. l7: the host 130 m away in Central Yharnam (Cathedral Ward's block unloaded: `object 2401102 not loaded`), the guest rode Cathedral Ward's lift 2401102 up (12400827 0 -> 1 on the guest, `kept local`), the host's 12400827 stayed 0; l8: the host walked back, the block's init event 12400823 put the car at the bottom (anim 30) while the guest's car was at the top: one game showed the car where the other had an empty shaft, and the car region / levers did nothing on the other. Fix: the guest's finished ride (busy flag off) goes up as `lift_set` / `lift_clr`; the host writes the position flag (and the newer lifts' host-state flag) only while the lift's block is not loaded in its game (`HostLiftSync`: same / busy / loaded are left to the game's own sync). l9: `guest: lift 2401102 ride over (flag 12400827 = 1, the host's world 0): to the host`, host `lift 2401102 12400827=1(applied: the lift's block is not loaded here)`, and at the stream-in the host's init took anim 0 (the top), the guest's end. Unit party-worldact-test (lift table of 12 lifts from the EMEVD, HostLiftSync, the request fields). **Ladder**: Central Yharnam's shortcut ladder 2411204 lowered by a guest's lever 2411316 (fixture: 12410285 cleared, l10): the host's game got the kick natively, anim 1 -> 2 and 12410285 = 1 on all three games (screenshots: no ladder, then the ladder down). **Illusory walls**: none in Bloodborne (no event of any map's EMEVD, Chalice templates included, is a hidden wall). 99 not 100: a kick-down ladder of the ladder system (2009[0]) not run (the raised ones in the test saves are already down, dlast); the "loaded but its copy did not follow" case is left alone (not seen live). 2026-10-16 dlast (live, `agents/dlast_shots_keep/s3`): **a lift ride by a guest** (Central Yharnam lift 2411320, 3 players): the guest stepped into the car at the top, the host 131 m away: the host's world ran the lift (its flags 12410350 and others 0 then 1, 0.16 s after the guest stepped in; the guest's own copies stay local, the lift's state is the host's), the host saw the guest ride -44.4 -> -58.0 -> -44.3 (remote chr positions), the guest's own position the same (log_pos; screenshot at the bottom). ## H. Bloodborne rule: a party that plays like single-player

| # | Feature | ERSC | bbport now | % | Evidence / next |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| H1 | No unnecessary loading screens | One load per warp; a death loads only the dead player | Same-map moves without a load (in-world); a lone traveller's load only for that player; one load per party travel | 100 | inworld1-2, split13-14; hostdeath hd10-hd20: a host death in the loaded map loads nobody (revive in place), in another map only the host. Open: a guest's lone death respawn is still a death load (the revive could serve it too) 2026-10-12 (0.5.6): stage warps inside the loaded map without a load (off by default, st1). 2026-10-12 (inworld, 0.5.9): scripted same-map warps closed as not applicable, `BB_PARTY_STAGE_INWORLD` stays off: an EMEVD scan (every 2003[14] and 2002[2/4/6] warp) shows all 12 warp points with a spot in their own map are cutscene warps (2002[4] / [6]: Gehrman ending 2102808/9, Cathedral Ward day to evening 2402200, boss intros m33/m34/m35), which never call the travel funnel (none of its 15 callers), so the hook never runs for them; the only same-block 2003[14] warps are Chalice layers (m29_02 2900980-89, no MSB spot, a new layout needs the load). No real warp reaches the switch, so turning it on would change nothing in play (split_travel.md §9) 2026-10-14 (playtest 1, user): a loading screen wins where the no-load path glitched: a host death is again one load for everyone (`BB_PARTY_B2_HOST_REVIVE=1` / `BB_PARTY_INWORLD=1` opt-in for the old no-load paths) and a guest's lamp of the loaded map is its own load (`BB_PARTY_GUEST_LAMP_INWORLD=1`); the host never moves for it 2026-10-15 (parceh, live): load count per event on the current build: party travel one load each (also at 250 ms RTT), guest death none, host death one each, a guest's alone rest = its own load + the host's rest load (nobody else), a rest during a boss fight one load (C7). The two loads ERSC would not show (a host death and a guest's rest load the host's world) are the playtest-1 decision ("a loading screen where seamless glitches"). 2026-10-15 (parceh2, live): a guest's repeated deaths each revive in place with no load (C1 fix; before, the second one became a leave, a load and a re-summon); the mirror's new look reaches the others with no load (H5). 2026-10-15 (parfin3, load inventory on the current build, live s3a / s3b, `load started` per game): every loading screen a party session still shows, against ERSC: **(1)** joining: one for the joiner (ERSC the same); **(2)** a party travel (lamp YES, Dream trip, story warps, chalice entry / exit): one per player (ERSC: only the traveller; here the others chose YES; NO is the traveller's own load only); **(3)** a host's same-map lamp: none (in-world, B4); a rest at the lamp the host stands at: none (`already standing at it: no warp, no load`); **(4)** a guest's death: none (revive in place; ERSC: a load for the dead guest); a wipe: one each (as ERSC); **(5)** a host's death: one each (decision, C2; ERSC: the host only); **(6)** a guest's alone rest: its own load plus the host world's rest load (D9 / playtest 1: in BB only a load resets the enemies; ERSC resets without one); **(7)** a guest's lamp of the loaded map: its own load (decision; `BB_PARTY_GUEST_LAMP_INWORLD=1` in-world opt-in); **(8)** a crash: the crashed game's boot and one load into the host's world (C6; the replay that turned it into two loads plus a re-summon is fixed); a host crash: the guests' home load and the load back. Nothing else loads (s3a: host 4 loads = start lamp, death, Dream, back; the guest one per party move; s3b: none for the rest after travelling). Every load ERSC would not show is (5), (6) or (7), each a playtest-1 decision ("a loading screen where seamless glitched") with its no-load path kept as an opt-in, so none can be removed correctly now; scored by those decisions. Seen on the way: a guest's party-travel load ends ~15 s after the host's (it starts when the host's world is up: one load, but a longer one). 2026-10-16 (seam1, 0.6.3): **load length** (research #1): bbhost's fast-loading idea built natively (`BB_FAST_LOADING`, opt-in): A/B in one session at the 60 FPS preset, the same load kinds: host death load 11.4 / 11.4 s on vs 12.3 / 12.3 s off, party lamp travel 2.0-2.1 / 4.7-4.8 s on vs 2.2 / 4.9 s off, guest follow loads 2.4-2.5 vs 2.6 s: 3-8 % shorter, because loads here are I/O and loader-thread bound, and unpaced the game drew no frames (its frame skip): the loading screen's animation stood still. Off by default (correct over seamless); the default `uncap` preset is unpaced in loads already. Party timing unaffected (all re-joins within 8-14 s of the warp). 2026-10-21 (parity071): playtest 2 (0.6.9): every guest death took a loading screen (0.6.9's always-load revive, and ordinary deaths read as falls); 0.7.0 brought the no-load revive back and fixed the misread, 0.7.1 the fall's safe floor, on one PC only. Parity 95 -> 88. 2026-10-22 (parity072): real-session reports: the no-load fall revive left the fall camera on and the ladder state. Fixed in 0.7.2 on one PC only. Parity 88 -> 86. |

- 2026-10-19 (issue4, 0.6.7+): D1 (session kept after a boss kill) fix, issue #4 item 1: after a boss kill none of the guest's action buttons worked (lamps, NPCs, pickups). Cause (RE + live): the boss-clear Lua states call `SetLoadWait` (0x13358F0), which sets the Lua event context's (`*(*0x553B0C8 + 8)`) +0x8D load-wait flag; the Lua event update 0x1339B50 copies it into SprjActionButtonMan +0x52 (the phantom's action-button hold) every frame, and only the context's constructor clears it. K10 drops the send-home load that would have rebuilt it, so the hold stayed for the whole session (pard3's item-box lift only covered an open box, and put the hold back after it). Now the guest clears the flag and the hold once K10 kept the party (`K10ActionHoldTick`; `BB_PARTY_K10_HOLD=keep` the old behaviour), and the item box no longer puts it back. Live A/B in one session (`agents/issue4/runs/r1c`, Gascoigne killed by the host, test aid `BB_PARTY_K10_HOLD_DELAY=40`, probe `BB_PARTY_K10_PROBE=1`): load wait 1 / +0x52 1 from the K10 on; Cross twice at the Tomb of Oedon lamp at K10+9 / +13 s: nothing (screenshot `pressA_1.5s_inst1.png`); lifted at K10+40 (1 -> 0, stays 0); Cross at K10+46 s: the guest kneels at the lamp and its warp menu opens (7 entries, `pressB_1.5s_inst1.png`), the next Cross chose the Dream and "Travel with the party?" showed. (r1 / r1b on the Cleric Beast: the lamp there is not drawn on the guest's screen after the kill, so no prompt either way; a write to +0x52 alone was back to 1 the next frame.) D1 stays 100.
- 2026-10-19 (issue4, 0.6.7+): C3 (party wipe) fix, issue #4 item 6: the host forgot a guest's death after 90 s (`DeadMemberCount` dropped the `member_down` notice, which is sent once per HP-0 period), so a guest that spectated a boss fight for over 90 s no longer counted when the host died: `1 member(s), 0 down`, the host went into boss limbo and both players stayed stuck. The record (`deathrules::DownLedger`) now lasts until the member is revived (`member_revived`), arrives again (`arrived`: a rejoin / respawn load) or leaves the roster, or its own vitals say alive for 3 s; members whose vitals say HP 0 also fill `down_seen` now (it was never set). Unit: party-death-rules-test (a 400 s spectator then the host's death = Wipe; revival, leave, lost-but-kept member, dead-again restart). Live (2 instances, Gascoigne fixture, `agents/issue4/runs/r6`): guest down at +139.9 s, spectating; host killed at +271.5 s (131.6 s later): `host death: every member is down too (1 of 1): a wipe`, one respawn load on each game, both back at the Tomb of Oedon lamp (screenshots `wipe_40s_inst0/1.png`). C3 stays 100.
